### [1] [WDM_GW_Wavelets: A fast, JAX-based Python implementation of the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer wavelet transform for the time-frequency analysis of gravitational wave data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17396)
*Christopher J. Moore,Tomasz Kinowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个基于JAX的Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）时频变换，用于引力波数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波数据分析需要在时频域对非平稳、非高斯噪声中的瞬态信号进行高效刻画，WDM变换因其良好的时频局部化特性被广泛采用；但现有实现多基于CPU/传统数值库，难以满足大规模批处理与硬件加速（GPU/TPU）需求。

Method: 利用JAX的可组合函数变换（jit、vmap、grad）与自动微分/向量化能力，将WDM变换（基于Daubechies-Meyer小波构造的过采样时频分解）实现为纯函数式、可编译的算子，从而支持在CPU/GPU/TPU上运行并支持批量并行。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；预期优势包括：可微性与JIT编译带来的加速、批量数据并行处理能力，以及与Python/JAX生态（如与基于JAX的引力波推断流程）的无缝集成。

Conclusion: 该工作为引力波时频分析提供了一个可移植、可加速、可微的WDM变换实现，为后续在GPU/TPU上进行大规模时频分析与端到端可微信号处理奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper describes a JAX-based implementation of the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) time-frequency transformation, intended for use in gravitational-wave data analysis.

</details>


### [2] [Axial gravitational-wave propagation and mass--gravity identifiability in conformal scalar--tensor lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17840)
*Z. F. Gao,W. Q. Ma,H. Wang,Z. J. Cao,N. Wang,G. Q. Jin,Luiz C. Garcia de Andrade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在无质量、非屏蔽共形标量-张量理论中，用弱自引力物质透镜的静态Einstein–标量外部解研究张量波传播，得到奇宇称主势的标量荷修正（R^-4，下一项R^-5含ℓ(ℓ+1)-9变号），并区分固定Einstein框架质量与固定测量质量的Schwarzschild基线，用WKB、零测地线弯折与数值积分检验相关极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察共形标量-张量理论中张量波通过物质透镜时的传播效应，特别是标量荷对奇宇称引力主势的修正、多极依赖及可观测色散/相位效应能否与透镜质量或标量耦合分别识别。

Method: 采用静态Einstein–标量外部解匹配弱自引力物质透镜；对奇宇称引力主势作径向展开并与精确外部势比较；联系局部归一化后牛顿光偏折参数；有限半径WKB展开；另取固定碰撞参数极限得到消色差几何光学作用量；用独立零测地线弯折与数值求积校核；对点透镜作双像Fisher矩阵分析，释放到达时间与常数相位 nuisance 参数。

Result: 奇宇称主势在固定Einstein框架质量下R^-4阶出现标量荷修正，R^-5系数正比于ℓ(ℓ+1)-9，在四极与更高多极间变号，与精确外部势对比验证；固定多极下四极的主导频率依赖势矩在有限半径WKB中抵消，固定碰撞参数极限给出消色差几何光学作用量及次主导轴向量贡献；消色差观测仅约束有效质量，不能分离透镜质量与标量耦合；双像Fisher计算给出释放nuisance参数后的差分相位可辨识度。

Conclusion: 标量-张量透镜效应主要表现为有效质量标度与高阶多极修正；色散信号受消色差几何光学极限压制，模型相关色散观测须依赖完整匹配的像传递函数；现象学相位实验应与作用量导出的传播量分开处理。

Abstract: We investigate tensor-wave propagation in a specified massless, unscreened conformal scalar--tensor theory using a static Einstein--scalar exterior matched to a weakly self-gravitating matter lens. The odd-parity gravitational master potential has a scalar-charge correction at radial order $R^{-4}$ for fixed Einstein-frame mass. Its next $R^{-5}$ coefficient depends on $\ell(\ell+1)-9$, reversing sign between the quadrupole and higher multipoles; we test the expansion against the exact exterior potential. We connect this result to the locally normalized post-Newtonian light-deflection parameter and distinguish the Schwarzschild baseline at fixed Einstein-frame mass from that at fixed measured matter mass. A finite-radius WKB expansion reveals cancellation of the leading frequency-dependent potential moment for the quadrupole at fixed multipole, whereas a separate fixed-impact-parameter limit recovers an achromatic geometrical-optics action and a subleading axial contribution. Independent null-geodesic bending and numerical quadrature check these limits. For a point lens, achromatic observables constrain an effective mass rather than the lens mass and scalar coupling separately. An illustrative two-image Fisher calculation quantifies differential-phase identifiability after arrival-time and constant-phase nuisance parameters are released. The phenomenological phase experiment is separated from action-derived propagation quantities; model-specific dispersive observations require a complete matched image transfer function.

</details>


### [3] [Complexity and symmetry: The asymmetry factor approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17940)
*L. Herrera*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究自引力流体球演化过程中瞬时出现类时Killing矢量（TKV）的影响，借助非对称因子使某种对称性仅在特定时间坐标值下存在；分析绝热与耗散流体的相关物理变量，揭示TKV出现的印记，并首次建立TKV与描述流体复杂度的复杂度因子之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨自引力流体球在演化过程中瞬时出现类时Killing矢量（TKV）所产生的影响，并厘清对称性与流体复杂度之间的关系，此前二者联系尚未被揭示。

Method: 引入非对称因子（asymmetry factor），使某一特定类型的对称性（如TKV）仅在给定的时间坐标值下存在；分别考虑绝热流体与耗散流体两种情形，分析演化过程中各相关物理变量的行为，并考察TKV与复杂度因子之间的关系。

Result: 对相关物理变量的分析显示出TKV在演化某一点出现时所留下的印记；并发现TKV与描述流体复杂度的复杂度因子之间存在关联，首次揭示了复杂度与对称性之间的联系。

Conclusion: 自引力流体球演化中TKV的瞬时出现可通过物理变量识别，且TKV与复杂度因子存在内在联系，从而首次将复杂度与对称性联系起来。

Abstract: We study the influence of the instantaneous appearance of a time-like Killing vector (TKV) in self-gravitating fluid spheres during their evolution. For doing so we resort to the concept of asymmetry factor which allows the existence of any specific type of symmetry (e.g. a TKV), exclusively, for a given value of the time-like coordinate. We consider adiabatic and dissipative fluids. The analysis of different relevant physical variables in this process shows the imprints of the emergence of TKV at some point of the evolution. Particular attention is paid to the relationship appearing between the TKV, and the complexity factor describing the degree of complexity of the fluid, bringing out for the first time a link between complexity and symmetry.

</details>


### [4] [Quasinormal modes of dyonic axion black holes with nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18117)
*Jialun Zhao,Antonio De Felice,Shinji Tsujikawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究含无质量轴子与非线性电动力学（NED）的静态带电黑洞准正规模。无NED修正时给出5个动力学自由度对应的5族振荡模式复频率，均由RN基模延续而来；轴子耦合破坏引力-电磁宇称等谱性。加入磁荷产生轴子毛发并耦合全部五种扰动，改变振荡频率与阻尼率，部分强耦合基准还出现纯虚增长模。NED方面，两类四阶相互作用在等系数时由电磁对偶使各分支一阶频移与电荷组成无关，不等系数时对偶约束同一RN宇称对内频移之和；阻尼率变化约1%，四阶项在外部远小于局域Maxwell能量密度。谱方法与积分-匹配法结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性电动力学与轴子场是超出Maxwell理论的重要推广，二者对黑洞扰动谱（等谱性、模式结构、稳定性）的影响尚缺乏系统研究，尤其是引力与电磁宇称等谱性破缺及NED修正的定量刻画。

Method: 采用谱方法计算QNM复频率，并以独立的积分-匹配（integration-and-matching）法交叉验证。考虑球对称带电背景上的无质量轴子与NED耦合，分别处理：无NED时五自由度系统的五族模式；有限轴子耦合下磁荷诱导的轴子毛发；以及由两个标准电磁不变量平方构造的两类四阶NED相互作用（等/不等四阶系数），借助电磁对偶分析一阶频移性质。

Result: 轴子耦合在纯电RN背景上即使无轴子毛发也使五个频率互不相同；非零耦合下Q/M=√7/4、ℓ=2时解耦RN极限已知的电磁-标量谱简并可解释线性频率分裂。磁荷使五扰动全耦合且改变频率与阻尼；强耦合基准出现纯虚增长模。等四阶系数时各引力/电磁分支一阶NED频移与电-磁电荷组成无关（固定Q/M）；不等系数时对偶仅约束每个孤立RN宇称对内频移之和。NED使阻尼率变化约1%；四阶项在外部始终小于局域Maxwell能量密度。两种数值方法结果一致。

Conclusion: 轴子耦合一般破坏引力-电磁宇称等谱性，并可与磁荷毛发共同显著改变QNM谱，甚至引入不稳定增长模；四阶NED修正虽数值很小（约1%量级阻尼改变），但受电磁对偶严格约束，可作为检验强场修正的敏感探针。谱方法是研究多自由度耦合扰动的高效可靠工具。

Abstract: We study quasinormal modes (QNMs) of static charged black holes with a massless axion and nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). Without NED corrections, we compute the complex QNM frequencies of five oscillatory mode families in the system of five dynamical degrees of freedom. These families are continued from the fundamental Reissner--Nordström (RN) modes. Axion coupling generically breaks gravitational and electromagnetic parity isospectrality. At representative nonzero couplings, all five frequencies are distinct even on purely electric RN backgrounds without axion hair. Adding magnetic charge sources axion hair, couples all five perturbations, and modifies their oscillation frequencies and damping rates. Additional purely imaginary growing modes occur in some strong-coupling benchmarks. The known electromagnetic--scalar spectral degeneracy at $Q/M=\sqrt{7}/4$ for $\ell=2$ in the decoupled RN limit explains the linear frequency splitting induced by a small axion coupling. We include two quartic NED interactions obtained by squaring the two standard electromagnetic invariants. With the axion decoupled and equal quartic coefficients, electromagnetic duality makes the first-order NED frequency shift of each individual gravitational or electromagnetic branch independent of electric--magnetic charge composition at fixed $Q/M$. For unequal coefficients, duality instead constrains the sum of the first-order NED frequency shifts within each isolated RN parity pair. Depending on the coefficients, NED increases or decreases the damping rates by about one percent in representative backgrounds, while the quartic terms remain small compared with the local Maxwell energy density throughout the exterior. The QNM frequencies and NED shifts obtained with the spectral method agree with independent integration-and-matching results at representative parameter values.

</details>


### [5] [Separability of coupled gravitational perturbations in Plebański--Demiański spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18200)
*Finnian Gray,Hryhorii Ovcharenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将Hintz及后续工作提出的Kerr–Newman型引力扰动可分离性方法推广到Einstein–Maxwell方程最一般的type D电真空解（含对齐物质与宇宙学常数），利用方程的共形性质构造手征系统并找到实现解耦的对称算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Kerr–Newman及Kerr–Newman-(A)dS的手征可分离方法多依赖"冻结近似"（忽略与电磁自由度的耦合），或以耦合方程组形式处理引力辐射自由度；本文希望摆脱近似、并覆盖更广的解族，扩展到最一般的type D电真空解，同时纳入对齐物质与宇宙学常数。

Method: 把耦合的引力—电磁扰动方程组变换为手征形式，使其适用于改进的矩阵R-分离变量；借助方程的共形性质进行构造；识别出手征系统解耦背后的对称算符。

Result: 成功在含对齐物质与宇宙学常数的最一般type D电真空解中完成该构造，握手征形式的矩阵R-可分离性，并找到一个支配手征系统解耦的对称算符。

Conclusion: type D电真空背景下的引力扰动可分离性可统一处理，不依赖冻结近似；共形性质与对称算符是推广的关键工具，为一般化Teukolsky型分离变量提供框架。

Abstract: Recently, a major breakthrough in the separability of gravitational perturbations of the Kerr--Newman rotating charged black hole was achieved by Hintz [arxiv:2609.33661], and extended to non-zero cosmological constant Kerr--Newman-(A)dS black holes by Yoshida [arxiv:2610.20500] and Zhao [arxiv:2610.22274]. This method removes the need for the freezing approximation, wherein coupling to the electromagnetic degrees of freedom are ignored, at the price of a coupled set of equations in the gravitational radiative degrees of freedom. The key insight is to transform the coupled system of equations into a chiral form which is amenable to a modified matrix $R$-separation of variables. In this work we extend the construction to the most general type D electro-vacuum solution to the Einstein-Maxwell equations, with aligned matter and cosmological constant. The construction makes use of the conformal properties of the equations and we find a symmetry operator underlying the decoupling of the chiral system.

</details>


### [6] [Notes on the singularity theorems of general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18398)
*Georgios Papadopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 这些讲义从时空局部几何出发，研究其全局因果结构，并据此给出Hawking宇宙学奇点定理与Penrose黑洞奇点定理的教学式但技术性的证明，同时介绍证明所需的数学工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为奇点定理提供一个既具教学性又保持技术严谨性的证明，帮助读者理解局部几何、全局因果结构与奇点定理之间的逻辑联系，并配套图示与实例降低理解门槛。

Method: 先建立时空局部几何，再分析全局因果结构；以此为基础证明Hawking宇宙学奇点定理与Penrose黑洞奇点定理。文中使用大量图示与实例，并系统阐述曲线及其空间的拓扑与解析性质、Arzelá–Ascoli定理、半连续函数的性质，以及部分证明Hopf–Rinow定理（Riemann流形中测地完备性的条件）。

Result: 给出了Hawking与Penrose奇点定理的完整证明；系统整理了曲线空间、Arzelá–Ascoli定理与半连续函数等数学工具；并部分演示了Hopf–Rinow定理。

Conclusion: 该讲义的贡献主要在于教学与知识整合，将局部几何、全局因果结构与奇点定理的证明串联起来，并配以图示、实例与数学工具说明，适合作为学习奇点定理的技术性入门材料。

Abstract: The notes begin with an exploration of the local geometry of spacetime and proceed with an investigation of its global causal structure. The results obtained are used to present a proof of the Hawking cosmological and the Penrose black hole singularity theorems. The aim is to give a pedagogical, but still technical, proof of the statements. The notes include a large number of drawings to aid the understanding of the material and many examples to explain key concepts. Some of the mathematical tools utilised in the proof of the singularity theorems are also illustrated. These include an extensive description of the topological and analytic properties of curves and their spaces, of the Arzelá-Ascoli theorem and of the properties of semi-continuous functions. The Hopf-Rinow theorem, which describes the conditions for geodesic completeness in the context of Riemannian manifolds, is also partially demonstrated.

</details>

### [7] [Analytic spread complexity in three-level systems and the spacing-ratio distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17417)
*Pratik Nandy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以三能级系统为最小示例，精确求解了Krylov基下的扩散复杂度、Krylov方差与Krylov熵，并给出Gaussian系综下仅依赖相邻能级间距比分布的普适晚时行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散复杂度被提出用于刻画能级间关联，但此前主要依赖对大矩阵的数值研究，缺乏对能级间距关联如何体现在动力学中的解析理解，尤其缺少可精确求解的最小模型。

Method: 选取具有间距关联的最小体系——三能级系统，解析推导无限温度下的Krylov基、扩散复杂度、Krylov方差与Krylov熵；对Gaussian系综利用谱方差与相邻间距比的统计独立性，将系综平均量化为对间距比分布的一维积分，并推广至d能级情形。

Result: 无限温度下Krylov基仅通过相邻能级间距比依赖谱，谱方差只作为整体时间尺度；Gaussian系综中二者统计独立，平均复杂度与方差化为一维积分。得到复杂度和方差的普适晚时值，以及指数由Dyson指标决定的代数弛豫；d能级时平均复杂度表示为相邻间距比联合分布上的(d-2)维积分。

Conclusion: 能级统计（间距比分布）直接决定Krylov空间动力学的晚时行为与弛豫指数，三能级模型提供了可精确求解的基准，且方法可系统推广到d能级。

Abstract: The spread complexity characterizes the spreading of a quantum state over the Krylov basis and has been proposed to capture the correlations between energy levels. How such correlations manifest in the dynamics has been studied mainly numerically for large matrices. Here we consider three-level systems, the smallest setting with spacing correlations, and derive exact results for the spread complexity, the Krylov variance, and the Krylov entropy. At infinite temperature, the Krylov basis depends on the spectrum only through the ratio of consecutive level spacings, and the spectral variance enters only as an overall time scale. For Gaussian ensembles, these variables are statistically independent, and the averaged spread complexity and variance reduce to one-dimensional integrals over the spacing-ratio distribution encoding the level statistics. We establish universal late-time values of the complexity and variance, and an algebraic relaxation with exponents set by the Dyson index. We also outline the extension to $d$ levels, with the ensemble-averaged spread complexity expressed as a $(d-2)$-dimensional integral over the joint distribution of consecutive spacing ratios.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum violation of Causal Friendliness in the instrumental scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17431)
*Emanuele Polino,Giulia Lelli,Aaditya Datar,Marwan Haddara,Davide Poderini,Giorgio Milani,Sergei Slussarenko,Howard M. Wiseman,Nora Tischler,Eric G. Cavalcanti,Fabio Sciarrino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将局域友善性（Local Friendliness）推广到允许通信的仪器（instrumental）网络，提出"因果友善性"（Causal Friendliness）判据，并理论与光子实验证明量子关联与之不相容，揭示出比贝尔违反和经典因果违反更强的一类量子非经典性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔不等式违反表明无法同时保留观测事件的绝对性、自由选择、相对论因果性与经典因果解释；广义因果性可解释贝尔关联，但局域友善性定理显示维格纳朋友场景下即便放弃经典因果仍存在冲突。然而局域友善性此前仅限于贝尔因果结构，其在更一般因果网络（尤其允许通信的仪器网络）中是否持续存在尚不清楚。

Method: 构造仪器网络的维格纳朋友版本，该结构中各方之间允许通信、相对论因果性不再施加限制；在此框架下定义"因果友善性"，即观测事件绝对性与网络所施加的广义非经典因果约束之合取，并将相对论因果性从贝尔场景推广到任意结构网络。理论推导量子关联对因果友善性不等式的违反，并开展原理性光子实验进行验证。

Result: 理论证明并实验观测到量子关联与因果友善性不相容，即该仪器网络中的量子关联无法由满足绝对性与网络广义因果约束的模型解释；该结论强于此前仪器网络中对经典因果性的违反。

Conclusion: 将局域友善性从贝尔-维格纳场景推广至允许通信的仪器网络，确立了一种更强形式的量子非经典性，并揭示出一类新的关联，有望应用于未来量子技术。

Abstract: How far can quantum theory depart from a classical worldview? Bell inequality violations show that no theory can simultaneously uphold absoluteness of observed events, freedom of choice, relativistic causality, and classical causal explanations. While generalized notions of causality, capable of incorporating quantum resources, can account for Bell correlations, the Local Friendliness theorem showed that, in extended Wigner's friend scenarios, the conflict between quantum theory and causal explanations persists even after classical causality is abandoned. This revealed a new form of nonclassicality stronger than what was revealed by Bell's theorem. However, Local Friendliness has been confined to Bell causal structures so far, and it remains unknown whether a related form of nonclassicality persists in more general causal networks. Sang, PRX Quantum 7, 033052 (2026)], which approximately connect states within the same phase but have depths that grow logarithmically with system size. However, modeling open quantum systems driven by propagating quantum pulses becomes cumbersome once several components, channels, and temporal modes are involved. Here we present QuantumInputOutput.jl, an open-source framework that automates this derivation process: first, symbolic expressions for the Hamiltonian and Lindblad operators are derived using predefined SLH rules and symbolic quantum algebra. Next, the obtained operators are converted to (time-dependent) numerical matrices or are used to obtain higher-order mean-field equations. The numerical expressions are presented in a form immediately suitable for solving the time evolution of the master equation and analyzing the output modes via correlation functions. At the level of linear input-output theory, this construction makes the deterministic response explicit while exposing the force noise and correlations associated with the auxiliary sector. We organize the allowed ancilla correlations into three regimes. In Regime-I, independent ancillas have unsqueezed, uncorrelated sensing and back-action quadratures. In Regime-II, arbitrary local sensing--back-action correlations are allowed, but the ancillas associated with different objects remain independent. The single-object CGN is then unrestricted, while the joint force-noise matrix obeys a determinant bound that is equivalent to the local classical-channel/no-entanglement bound. In Regime-III, cross-object correlations and a common nonlocal mediator are allowed. Abstract: The recursive quantum approximate optimisation algorithm~(RQAOA) shrinks an unconstrained combinatorial optimisation problem by eliminating one variable at a time, guided by correlations measured in variational quantum states. We extend this recursion to constrained problems by building on the quantum alternating operator ansatz, which restricts the evolution to feasible solutions through a feasible initial state and feasibility-preserving mixers. However, every elimination alters the feasibility structure, so that such a mixer for the original problem generally fails to be one for the subproblems. Previous extensions to Maximum Independent Set and Minimum Vertex Cover sidestep this with tailored reduction rules that keep every subproblem within the original problem class. We resolve this by identifying, for a given constraint structure, a broader problem class that is closed under all eliminations, and by efficiently constructing feasible initial states and feasibility-preserving mixers for every class member. We do so for monotone constraints, such as for the knapsack problem, for linear equality constraints with polynomially bounded weights, including Hamming weight constraints, and for combinations of equality constraints with a single inequality constraint, such as the cardinality-constrained knapsack problem. Conversely, we show that these restrictions cannot be lifted in general without leaving the realm of efficient constructions. Exact simulations on knapsack and vertex-weighted max-bisection instances demonstrate that our recursive scheme can outperform both its non-recursive counterpart and penalty-based QAOA and RQAOA.

Abstract: Scalable control remains a major challenge for superconducting quantum processors due to the wiring, thermal load, and hardware complexity associated with conventional microwave-based architectures. A framework for qubit control based on Single Flux Quantum (SFQ) digital electronics is presented. For transmon qubits, optimized control sequences are constructed from regular pulse trains framed by arbitrary subsequences (ramps), enabling DRAG-inspired leakage suppression in the discrete SFQ regime through genetic-algorithm optimization. The objective of the proposed approach is to demonstrate that coherent errors intrinsic to SFQ control can be reduced to a comparable level for a more scalable cryogenic control architecture. Memory requirements are reduced by storing only the optimized ramps, while a modular controller architecture supporting both regular and arbitrary pulse generation is proposed. The logical complexity of this architecture is estimated from the Josephson-junction counts of its constituent SFQ cells. The proposed approach therefore combines low coherent-control error with reduced memory overhead and provides a basis for scalable cryogenic SFQ control architectures.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: Dicke态的magic在几乎所有填充率下都是广延资源，且具有高度可用的magic态产量；但半填充处SRE很小并不意味着资源弱，说明SRE等单一度量不能完整刻画magic资源。Dicke态可理解为倾斜自旋乘积态的固定磁化投影，这一视角统一解释了上述现象。

Abstract: Dicke states are an elementary family of many-body quantum states, consisting of equal superpositions of all configurations of $L$ qubits with $n$ excitations. We show that their magic, as measured by the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), is extensive at every filling $f=n/L$ except half-filling, and we obtain the magic density analytically, together with the subleading terms. In the context of fault-tolerant quantum computation, we also investigate the yield of Dicke states, and find that they can produce on average an extensive number of CCZ states (or $T$ states with a catalyst) at any $0<f<1$. This yield is not self-averaging, but fluctuates from run to run by $O(L)$, as if it were set by a hidden phase shared by all qubits. At half-filling, the SRE grows only logarithmically, while the preparation cost, average yield and relative entropy of magic remain extensive, so that a small SRE does not necessarily signal a weak resource. These results can be understood by viewing the Dicke state as a fixed-magnetization projection of a product of tilted spins.

Abstract: Quantum computers need real-time classical processing to convert measurement data into feedback within device-specific timing constraints. One calibrated matched-filter lane classifies a $50\times50$ array in 12.5 $μ$s. An atom-loss model using a software reference planner evaluates a $35\times35$ target with a 60 s trap lifetime and 0.1% loss per moved atom. Removing an assumed 150 ms host round trip raises the predicted assembly success from 8% to 85% and reduces the wall-clock time per successful assembly from 19 s to 0.16 s. A GPU-based outer loop is proposed for refitting model parameters between runs. Its NVQLink transport is measured on the board: over 10 GbE to an NVIDIA DGX Spark, the median FPGA-to-host-to-FPGA round trip is 2.74 $μ$s for 128-byte messages forwarded by a CPU core. A separate 12-bit learned qLDPC kernel is placed and routed for the same FPGA target, with a derived latency of 0.84 ms per round for a $[[1122,148]]$ lifted-product code under code-capacity noise. A quantised GraphMP ranking kernel for the same code runs on the board with a median host-to-FPGA-to-host latency of 50.5 $μ$s with one shot in flight, excluding host-side BP+OSD post-processing. Together, these implementations illustrate a shared architecture with workload-specific models and timing requirements.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热演化在开放耗散系统中要求极慢驱动以维持准绝热 Gibbs 态，实际速度受限；作者希望在不破坏 GKSL 结构与热力学一致性的前提下加速这一过程，得到可周期运行的驱动协议。

Method: 以谐振子与量子自旋为原型，先在固定外场下制备 Gibbs 态，再绝热改变外场；为保持准绝热 Gibbs 态作为 GKSL 方程解，求解逆问题得到对哈密顿量与耗散子的非绝热修正；随后施加 fast forward-and-backward 时间重标定，导出 FF 化的哈密顿量与耗散系数间详细平衡条件（DBC）的对应形式。

Conclusion: FF 方法可在保持详细平衡与热力学一致性的条件下实现开放量子系统的加速绝热演化，但耗散系数正定性带来本质性的速度上限，且在低温下该上限受指数因子限制，构成对 FF 驱动实用性的基本约束。

Abstract: We present the fast-forward (FF) approach to adiabatic time evolution of an open dissipative quantum system within the framework of the Gorini-Kosaakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) equation. Taking a harmonic oscillator (HO) and a quantum spin as prototype systems under a rapid change of the driving field and in contact with the heat reservoir, we reveal the protocol which creates the non-equilibrium Gibbs state. After preparing the Gibbs state under the fixed external field in advance, we begin to change adiabatically the field. To keep a quasi-adiabatic version of the Gibbs state as the solution of GKSL equation, we solve the inverse problem to obtain the non-adiabatic corrections both to Hamiltonian and to the dissipator. Then we apply the fast forward-and-backward (abbreviated as FF for convenience) time-rescaling, obtaining the FF variants of both Hamiltonian and detailed balance condition (DBC) among dissipative coefficients. The present scheme generates the time-periodic Gibbs state as if a limit-cycle solution of the innovated GKSL equation under the time-periodic driving field. Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) enables information-theoretically secure key sharing between two distant parties. Recently, one promising QKD protocol which can distribute a secret key over long distances called twin-field QKD (TF-QKD) has been developed. However, it requires active compensation for experimental imperfections such as phase, polarization, and frequency fluctuations with expensive devices and this makes its experimental implementation challenging. Our protocol can remove these requirements by using Gaussian-modulated coherent states and digital signal processing which is widely used in recent digital coherent optical communication and experimental implementations of CV-QKD. For security analysis, we use a numerical optimization approach with a photon-number cutoff assumption. We propose a new set of constraints for numerical optimization which can efficiently constrain a highly non-Gaussian quantum state shared by two legitimate parties in our protocol. Abstract: This book introduces quantum electromagnetics to readers trained in classical electromagnetics, without assuming a systematic background in quantum mechanics. The central question is when classical descriptions suffice, and when the quantum states of matter or the field must be retained explicitly. Starting from familiar electromagnetic modes and material response, the discussion develops atomic dynamics, field quantization, and the interaction of quantized fields with quantum matter. Particular attention is given to how mode profiles, Green functions, and scattering matrices obtained from classical electromagnetic calculations enter quantum models. Atomic coherence, photon statistics, spontaneous emission, and cavity coupling are developed through derivations that identify the physical assumptions and limits of each description. Examples drawn from atomic clocks, Rydberg-atom receivers, and single-photon devices connect these concepts to excitation, propagation, and measurement in practical systems. Loss and noise are included in relating internal dynamics to measured signals and correlations. The final discussion examines how sensitivity and claims of quantum advantage depend on bandwidth, measurement resources, and the classical reference used for comparison. The aim is to help readers choose an appropriate model, carry out the calculation, and understand what its predictions mean for an electromagnetic measurement.

</details>

Abstract: A state over time (stote) pairs a state $ρ$ with a channel $\mathcal{E}$ as the Hermitian, generally non-positive operator $(ρ\otimes\mathbf{1})\star J_\mathcal{E}$. Our main result is a convergent outer semidefinite hierarchy with an explicit trace-norm Hausdorff bound on its trace-one slices. A complementary inner hierarchy retains all channels at each chosen pure-input direction. For $d_A\ge2$, its error is at most $2(d_A-1)\sin^2δ$, where $δ$ is the Fubini--Study covering radius; nested sets of $N$ directions give error at most $2π^2(d_A-1)N^{-1/(d_A-1)}$, independently of $d_B$. These bounds provide explicit size--accuracy guarantees and certified support-value bounds, without guaranteeing finite termination for exact boundary membership. For binary classical outputs, we obtain an exact SDP membership test, including indefinite output blocks and mixtures that are not single stotes. At faithful marginal the test reduces to a scalar inequality; every trace-one member of this sector with input marginal $ρ$ has a constructive realization using at most $\operatorname{rank}ρ$ pure-input stotes with binary measurement channels. Finally, we compute the exact isotropic boundary and prove $\mathrm{PPT}\subsetneq\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}\cap\mathrm{PSD}\subsetneq\mathrm{PSD}$ for equal local dimensions at least two.

Abstract: For non-hardware-native \textsc{MAX-CUT} instances, the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) can incur additional two-qubit depth overhead due to SWAP-based routing of interactions between non-adjacent qubits. We propose a hybrid quantum--classical workflow that reduces this depth by selectively removing interactions and subsequently recovers solution quality through classical optimization of the original \textsc{MAX-CUT} objective. We employ a Minimum Linear Arrangement (MinLA)-based vertex ordering to preferentially retain short-range interactions, thereby constructing a two-qubit depth-reduced surrogate QAOA ansatz. To compensate for the information lost through interaction removal, we develop classical \textit{recovery} strategies that refine QPU-sampled bitstrings directly on the original \textsc{MAX-CUT} objective. We develop two recovery strategies: signed-residue-based local search and bipartite-block optimization, where each conditional block problem is solved exactly using an $s$--$t$ minimum cut. Recovery is performed independently on each sampled configuration, ensuring monotonic improvement in cut value without additional QPU executions. We evaluate the workflow on \texttt{ibm\_kingston} for 120 distinct non-hardware-native instances comprising $6$-regular and Erdős--Rényi graphs with 40, 60 and 100 vertices. We verify that the surrogates substantially reduce transpiled two-qubit depth while staying within the prescribed two-qubit depth, and with recovery yield \textit{near-optimal} cuts on the original graphs. These results demonstrate the potential of hardware-aware interaction selection and classical recovery to extend QAOA to graph instances whose full cost Hamiltonians exceed practical circuit-depth budgets.

Abstract: We present a comparative analysis of three quantum versions of the kinetic uncertainty relation (KUR), which incorporate coherence-dependent corrections to the classical bound. Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases with prescribed entanglement properties are of fundamental interest in quantum information theory. In the interesting case of a bipartite Hilbert space of unequal prime dimensions, $\mathcal{H}_p\otimes\mathcal{H}_q$, with $p<q$ prime, the standard solution provides $p+1$ product bases. For any such dimension $d=pq$ we construct $p+1$ bases, such that $p$ of them are represented by complex Hadamard matrices, each equivalent to a Fourier matrix $F_d$, with all vectors maximally entangled. Second, finite-dimensional operator geometry determines the form of each affine functional. Third, admissibility identifies which normalised probability--state pairs are compatible with the full preference. We introduce behavioural state-independence as the natural Anscombe--Aumann interpretation of the idea that the identity of the states of the world should not affect how quantum consequences are compared. Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution offers a promising platform for scalable quantum networks through its compatibility with standard telecommunications technology and photonic integration. By introducing a heterodyne-measured ancillary mode into the entanglement-based source description, we incorporate additional source correlations into the security analysis that allow the Heisenberg uncertainty principle alone to provide a tight bound for the unknown correlations in a covariance matrix characterizing the whole system. Abstract: Ancilla qubits are routinely used to simplify quantum circuit construction and reduce implementation costs, but their use introduces the burden of designing proper uncomputation afterward. To alleviate this, prior work has developed automated uncomputation synthesis based on a computational-basis formulation of ancilla use. For more general circuits, it may be undefined, yield unintended functionality, or fail to synthesize uncomputation. It therefore falls short of providing a precise and general formulation of uncomputation beyond classical ancilla use.

Based on this specification, we define uncomputation as restoring the initial ancilla role while implementing the identity functionality. This perspective enables a simple Use-first strategy for automatically synthesizing uncomputation in cases both within and beyond the scope of prior approaches. We further develop a complete inference system for reasoning about specifications, together with a derivation-directed procedure for automatically synthesizing efficient uncomputation, enabling systematic uncomputation synthesis even in challenging cases beyond the scope of our automatic strategy.

Abstract: In equilibrium quantum thermometry, the precision of temperature estimation increases with the heat capacity of the probe thermometer. Here, we propose a quantum extreme learning-based framework for quantum parameter estimation which avoids the explicit construction of the likelihood function. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this approach using the transverse-field Ising model for estimating the external transverse field. Our results show that high estimation accuracy can be achieved using measurements at only a small number of training points. We further extend the framework to a two-time measurement protocol, in which measurement outcomes obtained at two distinct times are combined to enrich the feature space, thereby significantly enhancing the estimation accuracy. Our analysis shows that the estimation precision improves monotonically with the number of training points, while increasing the system size generally enhances the sensing performance, albeit non-monotonically.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The scalability of variational quantum algorithms is limited by barren plateaus, where gradients vanish exponentially in system size. This worst-case result raises fundamental questions about the trainability of quantum AI models. We present a major advance towards resolving this trainability question by answering whether a given quantum circuit has a barren plateau. We do this using an analytical framework that bounds the gradient variance for arbitrary input states $ρ$ and observables $\mathcal{M}$ by decomposing the unitary representation of the associated compact Lie group. When the circuit segments on both sides of a gate form $2$-designs, we obtain the variance exactly and prove a sufficient condition for trainability: if $ρ^{\otimes 2}$ and $\mathcal{M}^{\otimes 2}$ are sufficiently concentrated on the same irreducible invariant subspace of polynomial dimension, and the differentiated gate moves this subspace by an inverse-polynomial amount, then the gradient variance is at least inverse-polynomial. We further present a sampling algorithm that estimates the gradient variance from runs of the ansatz, without constructing the representation-theoretic projectors. Our algorithm decides whether the variance exceeds a tolerance $τ$ with the number of runs polynomial in $1/τ$, including cases in which the bounds are inconclusive. This resolves the question of the trainability of the circuit before expensive training procedures.

</details>

Abstract: Optical spectra of dissipative molecular systems depend on both complex resonance energies and the way each resonance couples to an optical probe. In a non-Hermitian description, these con- tributions are determined by complex residues involving left and right eigenvectors. We introduce a quantum protocol that reconstructs the resonance poles and their biorthogonal optical residues within a common spectral-filtering and measurement framework. A Hermitian dilation embeds the left and right eigenvectors into a zero-energy subspace. Gaussian-filtered correlators evaluated with unitary circuits, together with ratios of complex off-diagonal measurements, yield the residues with- out separate variational optimisation of the eigenstates. We demonstrate spectral reconstruction for dissipative plasmon-molecule models on IBM quantum hardware, resolving how probe-dependent modal contributions produce interference-induced doublets or accompany a separation of resonance energies. We also analyse the sensitivity of residue reconstruction near exceptional points. By mak- ing complex optical residues an explicit computational output, the protocol connects non-Hermitian eigenstructure to measurable optical response.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke超辐射峰值强度随发射体数 N 呈二次标度，因而对实际参与的发射体数目高度敏感；实验上 N 往往不固定——不同制备批次间存在涨落，发射过程中又因原子损耗而减少。既有解析理论多假定 N 固定，难以描述这些真实情形，故需将解析处理推广至可变数目。

Result: 发现数目涨落会增强辐射，且系综平均的发射轨迹可编码初始数目分布；在无法完全分辨分布时，仍可由光子计数重构二阶关联。对损耗情形，峰值强度标度由单一变量 g_r = r·t_SR 支配：g_r<1 时峰值仍随 N 二次增长；g_r>1 时呈现对数修正的次二次幂律；强损耗 g_r≫1 下过渡为线性标度。

Conclusion: 可变发射体数目下的超辐射可由Schwinger玻色子框架统一解析描述；数目涨落与原子损耗对发射动力学的影响可分别通过初始分布反演和单一标度变量 g_r 刻画，为实验数据的定量解释与关联测量提供理论基础。

Abstract: Dicke superradiance, the collective spontaneous emission of identical quantum emitters into a common reservoir, produces a burst with a characteristic delay $t_{\mathrm{SR}}$ and a peak intensity that scales quadratically with the emitter number $N$. This strong dependence makes the burst sensitive to how many emitters actually participate. In experiments, however, this number is rarely fixed: it fluctuates between preparations and decreases during emission through atom loss. Here we extend the analytical treatment of superradiance to variable emitter numbers using the Schwinger-boson mapping of the collective spin onto two bosonic modes. In this formulation, collective decay annihilates an atom in the excited-state mode and creates one in the ground-state mode. We find that number fluctuations enhance emission and that the ensemble-averaged emission trace encodes the initial number distribution. We show how to reconstruct second-order correlations from photon counting with finite detection efficiency and temporal resolution, even when the full distribution cannot be resolved. For atom loss at rate $r$, we derive exact results for the population dynamics and show that the peak intensity scaling is governed by the single scaling variable $g_{\mathrm{r}}=r t_{\mathrm{SR}}$. The peak remains quadratic in $N$ for $g_{\mathrm{r}}<1$, follows logarithmically corrected subquadratic power laws for $g_{\mathrm{r}}>1$, and crosses over to linear scaling under strong loss with $g_{\mathrm{r}}\gg1$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算机上高效编码经典数据需兼顾硬件连通性限制；现有QPIXL/QCrank等方法在设备耦合图上可能引入SWAP门与路由开销，且读出开销随规模增长。作者希望提出一种无需路由、读出受本地耦合度约束的通用编码方案。

Method: Tessa将地址量子比特与数据量子比特以棋盘方式（checkerboard）交叉分布于耦合图，使相邻数据量子比特仅共享部分地址量子比特；直接利用设备耦合图时无需SWAP门或路由，构造适用于任意二分（bipartite）耦合图；读出所需shot数由本地耦合度决定。

Result: 在IBM Phoenix与Fez量子计算机上，对多种数据实现最多1568个实数值的编解码实验，数据类型包括猫态（像素猫艺术）、多种像素艺术、音频、MIDI、DNA序列与蛋白质氨基酸序列。

Conclusion: Tessa提供了一种硬件感知、设备级通用且无需路由的经典数据量子编码/解码方法，兼顾耦合图适配性与读出效率，并在真实超导设备上验证了较大规模的实用性。

Abstract: We present Tessa, a hardware-aware, device-wide scheme to encode classical data on quantum computers. We use it to experimentally encode and decode up to $1568$ real values using IBM's Phoenix and Fez quantum computers on a variety of data. Tessa's construction is an extension of QPIXL and QCrank. However, it checkerboards address and data qubits across a coupling graph with neighbouring data qubits sharing only a subset of address qubits. When using the device's coupling graph no SWAP gates or routing is required, but the construction works for any bipartite coupling graph. The shots required for readout are determined by the local coupling degree. As examples, we have used cat states (pixel art of cats), various pixel art, audio, MIDI, DNA sequences and protein amino acid sequences.

</details>


### [64] [Nesting in additive manufacturing using quantum annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18447)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Nesting in powder bed fusion (PBF), an additive manufacturing (AM) process, determines the positions and orientations of parts within the build space and influences build height and build time. As the number of parts and feasible placements increases, the nesting problem becomes computationally demanding. Exact approaches are limited to small problem sizes as computational effort increases rapidly with the problem size, while heuristic and metaheuristic approaches involve a trade-off between solution quality and computation time. Quantum annealing (QA) offers an alternative for solving combinatorial optimization problems, but its application to nesting in AM remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a QA-based approach for nesting in AM to investigate its feasibility and its scaling behavior across three problem sizes. An iterative binary quadratic model based approach (IBQM) is developed to minimize build height while considering the geometric characteristics of AM parts. Geometric abstraction, iterative decomposition, and variable pruning are applied to address the computational complexity of the nesting problem. The approach is evaluated using three cases with increasing problem sizes and compared to a classical approach combining tabu search and simulated annealing (TBSA). Compared with TBSA, IBQM reduces the build height of the original parts by up to 13.32% and the computation time by up to 74.27%.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a unified framework for predicting local moment dynamics in high-dimensional many-body systems from short-time trajectories. Thanks to the locality of the dynamics, we construct the transition moment matrices by projecting the Markov operator onto low-order connected-cluster basis functions supported near the target observable. Exploiting the spatial decay of time-lagged correlations, {these matrices} can be approximated in sparse-plus-low-rank form using trajectory samples, with computational cost scaling linearly in the system dimension and the number of trajectories. The learning error can be further reduced by correcting the memory effect induced by the Galerkin projection. We numerically demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework in extrapolating the dynamics of various many-body systems, including Langevin and Glauber dynamics for classical systems as well as Lindblad dynamics for open quantum systems. In addition, for a class of single-site Glauber dynamics, we establish error bounds for sparse-plus-low-rank compression and uniform-in-time bounds for connected-cluster approximation and observable propagation with compressed transition moment matrices.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate quantum state transfer in the poly-bi-[8]-annulenylene lattice using isotropic Heisenberg and Hubbard models. For a 16-site lattice, reflection symmetry separates the single-excitation Hamiltonian into independent triplet and singlet sectors, allowing analytical determination of the energy spectra. Although perfect state transfer is forbidden for positive couplings, appropriately chosen coupling ratios enable pretty-good transfer with arbitrarily high fidelity. Remarkably, the triplet transfer probability is independent of the interchain rung coupling, while singlet transfer can be selectively enhanced or suppressed, enabling symmetry-selective quantum transport without time-dependent control. Extending the analysis to the Hubbard model, we show that the Heisenberg transfer dynamics emerge in the strong-interaction limit, while finite interactions delay all high-fidelity transfer peaks of a symmetry sector by the same fraction of their transfer time, set by the spectrum, and reduce the peak fidelity, even at the corrected time, by an amount that grows with the transfer time. These findings are relevant to future quantum-network architectures, where symmetry-controlled state transfer could provide a building block for connecting quantum memories and emitter-based interfaces within quantum nodes. Overall, our results demonstrate how molecular network geometry and symmetry can be exploited to engineer and control quantum-state transport in interacting spin systems, providing a theoretical framework for quantum information transfer in molecular-inspired quantum architectures.

</details>


### [67] [Dissipation as a Resource for Entanglement via High-Spin Mediator Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18489)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中介体（高自旋磁杂质/Mn自旋）的热混合态会限制两自旋量子比特间的纠缠产生。作者希望厘清：是否可以通过强相干驱动或共振驱动纯化中介体、从而增强纠缠？该问题关系到"噪声/耗散"在量子资源制备中的角色定位。

Method: 采用Lindblad主方程对"两个自旋量子比特—单个S=5/2磁杂质"体系建模；对比相干驱动（幺正演化）与耗散通道（光学泵浦循环）对中介体纯度和纠缠的影响；耗散通道速率取实测单Mn自旋光学泵浦的参数；以首次复现时的负度（first-recurrence negativity）作为纠缠度量。

Result: 1）任何相干驱动（无论强度或共振条件）都无法使中介体纯度超过其热值，原因是幺正演化严格保持态纯度；2）耗散通道可将纯度提升至光学泵浦循环所设定的上限 Tr[ρ_M²]≈0.8；3）纠缠对泵浦强度非单调——存在有限速率峰值后下降，因同一通道通过中介耦合向量子比特泄漏退相干；4）在实验泵浦速率下，单个80ns脉冲即可纯化中介体，极化保持时间比纠缠动力学长三个数量级；5）12K时负度由0.18升至0.64，30K以下仍高于0.6。

Conclusion: 耗散不应被视为需压制的噪声，而应作为实现鲁棒纠缠产生的核心资源；相干驱动在原理上无法纯化中介体，而光学泵浦型耗散通道在有限温度（≤30K）下可稳定支撑纠缠，为基于高自旋杂质中介的量子比特纠缠方案提供了依据。

Abstract: We study entanglement generation between two spin qubits mediated by a single, thermally mixed, high-spin ($S=5/2$) magnetic impurity, using the Lindblad master equation to compare coherent and dissipative control of the mediator. We show that no coherent drive, however strong or resonant, can purify the mediator beyond its thermal value --- a direct consequence of unitary evolution exactly preserving state purity --- while a dissipative channel purifies it up to the limit set by the optical pumping cycle itself, $\mathrm{Tr}[ρ_M^2]\approx0.8$. Modeling this channel with rates fixed by the measured optical pumping of a single Mn spin, we find that the entanglement it enables is not monotonic in the pump strength: it peaks at a finite rate and then decreases, since the same channel leaks decoherence to the qubits through the coupling that mediates their entanglement. At the experimental pumping rate, a single $80$ns pulse purifies the mediator, the resulting polarization persists for a timescale three orders of magnitude longer than the entangling dynamics, and the first-recurrence negativity rises from $0.18$ to $0.64$ at $12$K, remaining above $0.6$ up to $30$K. These results establish dissipation, not as noise to be suppressed, but as the very resource that makes robust entanglement generation possible.

</details>

Method: 基于费米子可表示性理论：把每个量子比特编码为两轨道上的一个费米子，通过要求所有格点占据数方差为零，证明可表示的量子比特边缘态构成N-可表示费米子约化密度矩阵集合的一个暴露面，从而把完整的费米子可表示性条件层级精确迁移到量子比特情形。

Abstract: Whether local reduced states are compatible with a global quantum state---the quantum marginal problem---is central to quantum information and simulation. Here we present a solution to the ensemble marginal problem for qubits based on the representability theory of fermions. Encoding each qubit as one fermion in two orbitals, we prove that the representable qubit marginals form an exposed face of the set of $N$-representable fermionic reduced density matrices, selected by the vanishing of every site-occupation variance. Complete hierarchies of fermionic representability conditions therefore transfer exactly to qubits. Applications to frustrated Heisenberg and transverse-field Ising rings show that the $(2,3)$-positivity conditions are exact at the Majumdar--Ghosh point and accurate to about 0.5% at criticality. Because the conditions hold for any quantum state, they also provide a framework for mitigating measurement errors on qubit-based quantum devices.

</details>

Method: 将问题建模为智能体式进化程序合成：以具备预训练知识与工具使用能力的大语言模型为智能体，在电路验证器（verifier）的反馈引导下迭代进化程序，该程序输出定义Clifford电路的描述，覆盖逻辑态制备、逻辑门设计、码切换、码增长等一组精选任务。

Abstract: Scaling quantum computing toward practical quantum advantage is currently limited by the efficiency of circuits that operate on information encoded by quantum error-correcting codes. The full stack of quantum computing encompasses several tasks: preparation of logical code states, implementation of logical gates, logical routines on encoded qubits, etc. Yet, these circuits are largely designed by hand for individual cases, with some recent works incorporating machine learning for certain aspects. We formulate this problem as agentic evolutionary program synthesis, wherein a large language model, leveraging its pretraining knowledge and tool-use proficiency, and guided by a circuit verifier, evolves a program that defines a Clifford circuit for a wide range of curated tasks such as logical state preparation, logical gate design, and logical routines such as code-switching, code growth, etc. Our approach consistently finds fault-tolerant circuits for all of these tasks, each verified by an exhaustive check over faults.

</details>

Conclusion: BSBS利用全光子数分布解决SBS的扩展性问题，展示近中期光量子计算在含噪中等规模器件上处理实际复杂流体建模任务的潜力。

Abstract: Boson sampling machines have become a fruitful tool for establishing new concepts, which connect fundamental physics with computer science applications. Here, we exploit the intrinsically high-dimensional correlations generated by the measurement outcomes of a multiphoton boson sampling machine and demonstrate how a reconfigurable architecture can be programmed to autonomously model the time evolution of nonlinear dynamical systems that describe complex fluid flows. By extending on the original scattershot boson sampling (SBS) scheme to include higher photon number components -- a scheme which we label bright scattershot boson sampling (BSBS) --- this scheme overcomes scaling issues inherent to SBS-based schemes by utilizing all randomly generated input photon number distributions. We illustrate the capability of this scheme for two complex fluid-flow models: an eight-dimensional Lorenz-type model of two-dimensional thermally driven convection and a 16-dimensional model of global atmospheric circulation. Using a digital twin of the BSBS architecture based on multiphoton statistics, we show that the photonic approach accurately reproduces the temporal dynamics of both systems while being resilient to photon losses of up to 50 per cent. We also verified that the BSBS case outperforms a comparable run with coherent light. Moreover, its prediction horizon exceeds that of a classical reservoir-computing model with the same query complexity. These results highlight the potential of near-term photonic quantum computing for addressing relevant real-world tasks on noisy intermediate-scale devices.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为量子模拟散射的态制备提供了新思路与新理解，并给出一套可控、可校准的波包制备与误差诊断方案。

Abstract: Probing real-time dynamics is essential for understanding non-perturbative scattering and interactions in quantum field theory. A common challenge in real-time simulation is preparing accurate asymptotic incoming states. Through efficiently solvable eigenvalue problems, we jointly optimize a superposition of single particle momentum eigenstates and a single site interpolating operator to maximize preparation fidelity from the interacting vacuum. This leads to a localized excitation in the interacting theory analogous to a position eigenstate. Weighted sums of the resulting interpolating operator over lattice sites prepare wave packets of arbitrary spatial extent. To ensure that this construction produces the correct continuum limit, relative phases among single particle momentum eigenstates in this superposition are calibrated using a relation derived from Lorentz symmetry. Analyzing single particle wave packet propagation through the position space profile reveals oscillatory Airy distortions, which originate from interference between saddle point contributions at different momenta that share the same lattice group velocity. These oscillations systematically weaken when approaching the continuum limit, and reliable regimes are identified as a function of simulation time and wave packet momentum profile. Two-particle scattering simulations in interacting scalar field theory are also implemented, revealing artificial inelasticity arising from coarse local field digitization or deviation from the Nyquist-Shannon sampling point. With these artifacts under control, present simulations provide emerging evidence for physical inelastic processes in $φ^4$ theory. The constructions introduced in this work may provide new understanding of state preparation for the quantum simulation of scattering.

</details>

### [72] [Learning and Generating Time Trajectories of Multidimensional Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17711)
*Zihan Xu,Changho Kim,Andy J. Nonaka,Yuanran Zhu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出多维统计信息神经网络（SINN）框架，用于学习并生成多分量随机过程的轨迹，通过一维PDF集合表征联合分布、自适应损失平衡及参考归一化验证，在耦合Duffing振子、FPU链与格点Lotka-Volterra模型上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有SINN仅在各类一维非马尔可夫过程上得到验证，而实际应用需要多维随机过程；多维情形下面临两大障碍：直接估计多元概率密度函数（PDF）随维度增长计算不可行，且刻画多维过程所需的统计目标数量大幅增加、损失函数难以平衡。

Method: 以确定性循环神经网络为核心，输入白噪声序列流，生成统计特性匹配目标随机动力学的轨迹集合。三项关键改进：(1) 用过程分量线性组合所构造的一维PDF集合来表征联合分布信息，规避高维PDF估计；(2) 采用自适应损失平衡策略，动态加权总损失各分量以适应更多统计目标；(3) 引入参考归一化验证框架，将统计偏差相对于独立目标集合间的有限样本变异性进行解释；并考察输入白噪声维度对性能的影响。

Result: 在由两个耦合Duffing振子构成的Langevin基准上评估所提方法，并进一步展示其在Fermi-Pasta-Ulam链的Langevin动力学以及格点Lotka-Volterra模型的动力学蒙特卡洛模拟中的适用性；结果显示多维SINN能够生成统计特性与目标相符的轨迹集合。

Conclusion: 多维SINN框架为建模多维随机动力学提供了一种实用途径，可扩展至真实世界的多分量随机过程应用。

Abstract: We present a multidimensional statistics-informed neural network (SINN) framework for learning and generating trajectories of multicomponent stochastic processes. SINN is built upon a deterministic recurrent neural network that takes streams of white-noise sequences as input and generates ensembles of stochastic trajectories whose statistical properties match those of the underlying stochastic dynamics. While SINN has been demonstrated for various one-dimensional non-Markovian processes, extending it to multidimensional processes is essential for real-world applications. Here, we present key developments toward this goal. First, because direct estimation of multivariate probability density functions (PDFs) becomes computationally challenging with increasing dimension, we represent joint-distribution information using collections of one-dimensional PDFs constructed from linear combinations of the process components. Second, to accommodate the larger set of statistical objectives required to characterize multidimensional processes, we adopt a self-adaptive loss-balancing strategy that dynamically weights the components of the total loss function. Third, we introduce a reference-normalized validation framework that interprets statistical discrepancies relative to the finite-sample variability between independent target ensembles. We also investigate the role of the input white-noise dimension in SINN performance. We evaluate the proposed approach using a Langevin benchmark based on two coupled Duffing oscillators and further demonstrate its applicability to the Langevin dynamics of the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam chain and kinetic Monte Carlo simulations of a lattice Lotka-Volterra model. Our results demonstrate that the multidimensional SINN framework provides a practical approach for modeling multidimensional stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [73] [ADfiRe: An empirical- and CFD-informed advection-diffusion-reaction thermal model for wildfire spread](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18012)
*Konstantinos Vogiatzoglou,Nikolaos Sdiros,Costas Papadimitriou,Konstantinos Ampountolas,Petros Koumoutsakos,Vasilis Bontozoglou*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出ADfiRe混合框架，将经验关联式与CFD参数化嵌入物理型对流-扩散-反应(ADR)模型，实现兼顾实时性与物理机理的野火蔓延预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时野火预报面临两难：快速模型缺乏物理相关性，高保真模型计算代价过高，尤其在非均质多相固体燃料上模拟火焰蔓延时难以兼顾两者。

Method: ADfiRe将经验关联式与CFD启发的参数化融入基于物理的ADR方程。主要特点包括：采用成熟燃料数据库、支持多燃料、可刻画任意地形效应；引入Byram数刻画风致火焰倾斜，区分浮力羽流主导与风主导的蔓延机制；显式求解热化学降解耦合过程及火焰前锋的热质输运；采用基于Arrhenius的两阶段非均质反应格式，耦合固体燃料热解与氧限制的炭/挥发分燃烧。

Result: 在五个有详细记录的Mediterranean灌木地野外尺度火烧实验上验证，模型在蔓延速率(ROS)与火线强度等关键指标上与实测定量吻合。

Conclusion: ADfiRe在保持计算效率的同时具备物理可解释性，可作为实时野火预报的可行框架；其多燃料、地形与风效处理能力提升了实际适用性。

Abstract: Real-time wildfire forecasting requires a trade-off between rapid yet physically relevant models and high fidelity but computationally costly models of flame spread over heterogeneous, multiphase solid fuel. The present study introduces ADfiRe, a hybrid framework that integrates empirical correlations and CFD-informed parameterizations into a physics-based advection-diffusion-reaction (ADR) formulation. The key features of ADfiRe are the use of established fuel databases, the capacity to handle multiple fuels, and a methodology to capture the effect of arbitrary topography. Furthermore, it includes the effect of flame tilting due to wind, thus accounting for the differences between plume- and wind-dominated fire propagation. ADfiRe resolves explicitly the coupled thermochemical degradation as well as heat and mass transport mechanisms of the advancing fire front. The wind field is modeled based on an appropriately defined Byram number. The model is validated against five well-documented experimental field-scale fires in Mediterranean shrublands, showing quantitative agreement with key metrics such as the rate of spread (ROS) and the fireline intensity, through a two-stage, Arrhenius-based heterogeneous reaction scheme coupling solid-fuel pyrolysis and oxygen-limited char/volatile combustion.

</details>


### [74] [CGTransformer: A Topology-Aware Autoregressive Framework for Crystal Generation and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18303)
*Zhennan Zhang,Shaobo Yu,Haoting Zhang,Yu Han,Zhiyue Guo,Junjie Wang,Hao Gao,Jian Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出CGTransformer，用图嵌入排序策略（ESG）把周期晶体转化为拓扑引导的一维序列，在固定模型容量下提升晶体生成与性质定向逆向设计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型可统一实现晶体生成与逆向设计，但性能依赖周期结构的序列化方式：基于坐标的序列化会使对称等价或结构相近的晶体得到不同原子顺序，引入与结构连接性无关的变异性。

Method: CGTransformer：自回归生成框架，采用商图（quotient graph）拓扑与重心嵌入（barycentric embedding）构建元素对称图（ESG），将周期晶体网络转换为拓扑引导的一维序列，保留关键局部与对称相关结构信息。

Result: 生成有效性高；在MP-20基线中单次晶体结构预测（CSP）匹配率最高（63.24%）；相同骨干网络对比下结构还原优于坐标排序；宽带隙半导体性质条件逆向设计中，ESG的S.U.N.率显著更高且目标性质对齐更强。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导的序列构建是在固定模型容量下改善晶体生成与性质定向设计的有效途径。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models offer a unified route to crystal generation and inverse design, but their performance depends on how periodic structures are serialized. Symmetry equivalent and closely related crystals can receive different atomic orders under coordinate-based serialization, introducing variability unrelated to their structural connectivity. Here, we present CGTransformer (Crystal Graph Transformer), an autoregressive generative framework that addresses this challenge via a graph-embedded sorting strategy. Using quotientgraph topology and barycentric embeddings, Element Symmetry Graph (ESG) converts periodic crystal networks into topology-guided one-dimensional sequences that retain key local and symmetry-related structural information. CGTransformer achieves high generation validity and the highest single shot crystal structure prediction (CSP) match rate among the compared MP-20 baselines (63.24%), while same backbone comparisons show improved structure recovery over coordinate-based ordering. Furthermore, we demonstrate property-conditioned inverse design for wide-bandgap semiconductors, where ESG achieves a much higher stable, unique and novel (S.U.N.) rate and stronger target-property alignment than the coordinate-based baseline. These results establish topology-guided sequence construction as an effective route to improving crystal generation and property-directed design at fixed model capacity.

</details>


### [75] [A Surrogate Boundary Condition for Improved Accuracy and Performance of Local Modeling in Radiation Transport Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18535)
*Jonathan Trieloff,Ethan Peterson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息神经网络代理边界条件，替代真空/反射边界，用于端口插件的局部蒙特卡罗中子学建模，在保持物理合理性的同时避免全模型输运，通量预测误差小于12%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克CAD几何复杂，全模型中蒙特卡罗中子输运计算代价极高，端口插件设计迭代不可行。局部建模虽可降低成本，但通常假设真空或反射边界，对全局输运物理的近似较差，导致精度损失。

Method: 训练神经网络作为代理模型，复现端口插件表面对应于周围几何返回粒子的边界条件（物理信息边界条件）；仅在部件内部执行蒙特卡罗输运，保留周围区域的近似物理行为，无需网格拼接与全模型输运即可迭代部件设计。以简化基准几何对比真空边界、反射边界与全局输运结果。

Result: 代理模型的通量预测在任意能量 bin 内与全局输运解的偏差均小于12%，精度优于真空边界与反射边界；在简化基准几何上，代理边界条件的计算性能优于全局输运。

Conclusion: 神经网络代理边界条件可有效提升局部建模的物理保真度与计算效率，为端口插件等部件的快速设计迭代提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Monte Carlo neutronics analysis of tokamak designs is computationally expensive due to the complex CAD geometries required to accurately model them. Design iteration of port plugs using global transport in the full model is impractical because of the computational cost. To circumvent this, local modeling methods are used. In the local modeling method, a boundary condition is assumed on the port plug, such that particles that leave the port plug do not require transport in the surrounding global model. For this purpose, vacuum and reflective boundaries are assumed, both of which provide poor approximation of the true transport physics in the global model. To improve on current techniques, a surrogate model is presented that provides a physics-informed boundary condition for the component. A neural network is trained to reproduce the boundary condition on the component that represents the behavior of particles returning from the surrounding geometry. Monte Carlo transport is only necessary within the component, and approximate physics from the surrounding region are retained. This approach allows the design in the component to be iterated without requiring full model transport and joining of the component mesh to the global mesh in each iteration. In this work, the surrogate model is evaluated against vacuum and reflective boundaries-- outperforming both in accuracy, with surrogate predictions of the flux in the port plug differing less than 12% from the global transport solution in any given energy bin. The surrogate boundary condition is also demonstrated to be more performant than global transport for the simplified benchmark geometry.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Alignment with Reflect-Reasoning: Learning How To Follow Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17398)
*Henry Bell,Caroline Zhang,Dhaval Potdar,Mohammed Mobasserul Haque,Brandon Fain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Reflect-Reasoning 训练方法，使模型学会"如何推理以对齐原则"而非仅记住"遵循哪些原则"，从而在推理时可泛化到新颖、域外的原则体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 对齐所需的规范与原则高度依赖文化语境与部署场景，但现有对齐技术多在训练时固化人类偏好或宪法原则，部署者只能靠 system prompt 在推理时施加情境约束。已有的"生成后反思—批评—修订"方法优于单纯 system prompting，却受限于模型自身固有的推理能力，无法将原则对齐作为一种可迁移能力。

Method: Reflect-Reasoning：一种训练方法，教授模型对"如何对齐原则"进行推理（即原则对齐的推理过程），而非直接学习具体原则内容；与 system prompting 及显式提示的 in-context 反思推理策略作对比；在五个模型上评估，并迁移到医疗、金融等完全分布外的原则域，同时检验严重原则违背的罕见事件、安全工程是否受损及对抗性宪法越狱鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个模型上，Reflect-Reasoning 一致优于 system prompting 与显式提示的 in-context 反思推理；改进可泛化到医疗与金融等 OOD 原则域；降低罕见但严重的原则违背发生频率；不削弱模型原有安全工程，且对利用对抗性宪法的 jailbreaking 尝试具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Reflect-Reasoning 是将宪法对齐训练为一种可泛化推理能力的可扩展方法，使模型无需额外微调即可适配新颖原则并输出透明的推理轨迹。

Abstract: The appropriate governing norms and principles for the alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) often depend on cultural context and deployment application. However, most alignment techniques focus on training models to conform to human preferences or constitutional principles specified at training time and subsequently fixed. In practice, many deployers rely on system prompting to implement contextual norms and guardrails at inference time. Prior work has shown that post-generation reflective reasoning with critique and revision can improve conformance compared to simple system prompting, but such methods are limited to the reasoning abilities inherent in the trained model. We introduce Reflect-Reasoning, a training method that teaches models how to reason about alignment to principles, rather than simply which principles to follow. Across five models, Reflect-Reasoning consistently outperforms both system prompting and explicit prompting strategies for in-context reflective reasoning. These improvements extend and generalize to entirely out-of-distribution domains of medical and financial principles. We additionally demonstrate that Reflect-Reasoning reduces the occurrence of rare but serious principle violations. Finally, we show that Reflect-Reasoning does not erode the safety engineering of a model and is robust against jailbreaking attempts using adversarial constitutions. Overall, our results demonstrate that Reflect-Reasoning is a scalable method for training constitutional alignment as a generalizable reasoning capability, enabling models to adapt to novel principles and produce transparent reasoning traces without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [77] [Generative Design with Real-time Physics Explainability: Towards Product Definition in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17560)
*M. de Campos,C. Drygala,E. Ross,A. Bruce,M. Bohon,H. Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将可逆神经网络（INN）逆向设计与生成式物理仿真代理模型统一的生成式设计范式，并引入不确定性量化与经典工具校验，在翼型与燃烧室两个交互式环境中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械工程中存在两条独立的生成式AI研究路线：基于INN的逆向设计（从性能标签推断设计参数）与生成式AI作为物理仿真的实时代理模型。二者尚未整合；同时生成式设计易产生"幻觉"，可能给出物理不可行的设计。

Method: 构建统一框架：用INN从客户需求侧驱动设计过程，用系统物理的生成式仿真提供"为何该设计满足需求"的可解释性层；采用不确定性量化以抵御物理不可行的请求；并以经典仿真/分析工具进行校验。

Result: 开发并演示了两个交互式设计环境：一个基于仿真器数据的翼型设计环境，一个基于实测数据的燃烧室设计空间探索环境，用以展示该生成式设计范式的可行性。

Conclusion: 将INN逆向设计与生成式物理仿真代理模型结合、辅以不确定性量化和经典工具验证，可构成面向需求驱动、可解释且可信的机械工程生成式设计流程。

Abstract: In this article, we explore opportunities from using generative AI in mechanical engineering. On the one hand, generative methods based on invertible neural networks have been recently proposed for the solution of inverse design problems, allowing to infer design parameters from performance labels. On the other hand, generative AI models have shown that they can act as real time capable surrogates for physics based simulations. Here we suggest that these two streams should be combined in a unified framework, where INN are used to control the design process from the customer requirements perspective and the generative simulation of the systems physics adds a layer of explainability, why the proposed design fulfils the requirements. As generative design is prone to hallucinations, we propose uncertainty quantification to fend off physically infeasible requests. Finally, validation with classical tools is a requirement for generative design process. To demonstrate the capabilities of our novel generative design paradigm, we develop two interactive environments, one for airfoil design based on simulator data and one for the combustor design space exploration based on measured data.

</details>


### [78] [WETW: Learning Pair-Specific Weighted Euclidean Distances for Efficient DTW Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17412)
*Christos Panourgias,Qitong Wang,George Tzagkarakis,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WETW（学习式成对加权欧氏距离）及WETW-FV过滤-验证流水线，实现近似但距离精确的DTW相似性搜索，比最快精确方法快11.4倍（recall@10=0.93）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTW是时间错位时间序列相似性搜索的标准距离，但代价为序列长度的平方，且现有下界剪枝效果差，大规模长序列搜索缓慢。既有学习式近似对所有序列对使用同一固定规则（如欧氏距离）比较嵌入，而DTW对每对序列的对齐方式不同，二者不匹配。

Method: 观察到一对序列对齐后，DTW即是在该对所选坐标上的欧氏距离。据此提出WETW：用小型对称网络为每个嵌入维度及每个序列对预测权重（无需计算对齐），自适应地比较定长嵌入；嵌入由WESEE编码器产生，其多尺度时序特征提取器保留全局池化丢失的时间信息。训练上指出仅回归DTW距离不足，加入排序正则化。WETW-FV先用WETW构建候选清单，再用精确DTW验证。

Result: WETW将同一嵌入上最佳静态距离的误差减半；WESEE将recall@1由0.90提升至0.95；排序正则化将recall@1由0.79提升至0.95。在八个数据集上，WETW-FV比UCR Suite、下界方法与MESSI-DTW中最快的精确方法快11.4倍，平均recall@10为0.93，而最佳学习基线在相同速度下仅0.59；在48K点序列上仍比UCR Suite快40倍，recall@10为0.91。

Conclusion: 将DTW视为成对坐标选择上的欧氏距离，可让学习式嵌入比较具备成对自适应性；配合过滤-验证框架，在保证返回距离精确的前提下大幅加速DTW相似性搜索，显著优于学习式基线与经典精确方法。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) is the standard distance for similarity search over time series that are misaligned in time, but its cost is quadratic in the series length and lower bounds often prune few candidates, so search over large collections of long series is slow. Learned approximations embed the series to make comparisons cheap, yet compare every pair with the same fixed rule, usually the Euclidean distance, although DTW aligns each pair differently. We observe that, once a pair is aligned, DTW is a Euclidean distance over coordinates selected for that pair. Building on this view, we propose Weighted Euclidean Time Warping (WETW), a learned distance that adapts the comparison of fixed-length embeddings to each pair. A small symmetric network predicts one weight per embedding di- mension and pair without computing an alignment, which halves the error of the best static distance on the same embeddings. The embeddings come from the Weighted Euclidean SEries Encoder (WESEE), whose multi-scale Temporal Feature Extractor keeps the timing information that global pooling removes and raises recall@1 from 0.90 to 0.95, although WETW works with any encoder. We also show that fitting DTW distances is not enough for search, as regression alone misorders the closest neighbours, and that ranking regularization raises recall@1 from 0.79 to 0.95. Our filter-and-verify pipeline, WETW-FV, uses WETW to build a shortlist and exact DTW to check it, so the search is approximate but every returned distance is exact. Over eight datasets, WETW-FV is 11.4x faster than the fastest exact method among the UCR Suite, lower bounds and MESSI-DTW, at a mean recall@10 of 0.93, while the best learned baseline reaches 0.59 at the same speed. On 48K-point series, WETW- FV remains 40x faster than the UCR Suite at a recall@10 of 0.91.

</details>


### [79] [Classification with Large-Step Gradient Descent: Unifying Self-Stabilization and Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17459)
*Salma Tarmoun,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,Konstantinos Emmanouilidis,Ziqing Xu,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将统一的几何视角从过参数化最小二乘推广到指数尾损失（logistic、交叉熵）可分线性分类情形，引入曲率定义的参考子空间替代极小点流形，给出迭代（而非仅损失）的显式界，并揭示大步长下的瞬态隐式偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中的梯度下降常使用超过经典稳定阈值的步长，此时损失非单调、振荡，却往往收敛更快、泛化更好；现有理论三种视角各有局限：自稳定机制缺乏收敛速率、特定损失的严格速率未解释稳定机理、统一几何视角依赖极小点流形而分类问题中该流形不存在。

Method: 针对指数尾损失（可分线性分类下的logistic与多类交叉熵），构造由曲率定义的参考子空间以替代极小点流形，得到耦合动力系统：正交振荡被沿锐度下降方向的平行进展所阻尼；在正则性条件下（对对称数据模型上的logistic与多类交叉熵进行验证）推导迭代量的显式界，覆盖不稳定与稳定两种步长区间。

Result: 得到覆盖不稳定与稳定区间的迭代显式界，并在稳定区间识别出三阶段阻尼结构；同时发现大步长诱发瞬态隐式偏置：GD渐近收敛于最大间隔方向，但大步长可留下持续的正交残差，在特定几何下甚至被放大，从而影响有限时间内的最大间隔对齐。

Conclusion: 参考子空间可成功替代极小点流形，使统一几何分析适用于损失下确界不可达的分类设定；大步长带来三阶段阻尼与瞬态隐式偏置，说明有限时间对齐最大间隔方向与渐近结论可能不一致。

Abstract: Neural networks are often trained with gradient descent (GD) using step sizes that exceed classical stability thresholds, a regime where standard convergence theory no longer applies. Despite exhibiting non-monotonic loss and oscillatory dynamics, this regime frequently leads to faster convergence and better generalization. Existing theoretical perspectives offer complementary but incomplete insights: one identifies a self-stabilization mechanism that drives GD toward flat regions but does not yield convergence rates; another derives rigorous rates for specific losses but does not explain the underlying stabilization mechanism; and a third unifies these views for overparameterized least squares, but relies on the existence of a minimizer manifold, which is absent in classification settings. In this work, we extend this unified geometric perspective to exponential-tail losses, including logistic and cross-entropy losses in separable linear classification, where the loss infimum is not attained at any finite parameter. We identify a curvature-defined reference subspace that replaces the role of the minimizer manifold, yielding a coupled dynamical system in which orthogonal oscillations are damped by parallel progress along directions of decreasing sharpness. Under regularity conditions, which we verify for logistic and multiclass cross-entropy losses on symmetric data models, we derive explicit bounds for the iterates, not just the loss, covering both the unstable and stable regimes, and revealing a three-phase damping structure in the latter. Our analysis further uncovers a transient implicit bias induced by large step sizes: although GD converges asymptotically to the max-margin direction, large steps can leave persistent orthogonal residuals and, in certain geometries, amplify them, affecting finite-time alignment with the max-margin direction.

</details>


### [80] [Unfinished-Bench: Measuring Rule Violations by Persistent Agents in Realistic Work Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17460)
*Seunghyun Lee,Jaehui Hwang,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Unfinished-Bench 基准，在无用户监督的自主长程执行场景中评估"持久性"对指令-规则冲突下违规行为的影响，发现持久性显著提升对内部文档规则的违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令-规则冲突基准要么把规则明写在提示中，要么完全隐藏规则，且均未评估"持久性"（为完成任务而持续推进）。而现实中智能体被赋予长程自主完成委托工作的能力，当完成目标需要违反内部文档或他人授权中的隐性约束时，智能体必须自行决策，这一风险缺乏评测手段。

Method: 构建 Unfinished-Bench：在嵌入规则的真实工作环境中，以无用户的自主运行方式赋予模型持久性。共 97 个场景，覆盖 7 个组织、7 类风险类别，每个场景中目标与规则无法同时满足。通过对比持久性与非持久性条件，以及规则来源（内部文档 vs. 用户明示）来量化违规率。

Result: 持久性使内部文档规则的违规率从 32.2% 升至 43.6%，是同一条规则由用户陈述时 8.5% 的五倍。文档规则违规中 98.3% 发生在规则已被展示之后，且在裁判抽样中有 89% 的案例智能体首先写下了该规则。

Conclusion: 智能体在缺乏用户监督时对环境中规则的违规可导致严重事故，持久性驱动的风险必须被纳入评测；"看到并记录规则"并不能阻止违规，说明现有对齐手段不足。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly equipped with persistence toward task completion to run delegated work autonomously for long periods, under many unstated constraints from internal documents and other people's authority. When completion requires violating a rule, the agent must decide alone, yet benchmarks of instruction-rule conflicts state the rule in the prompt or keep it hidden and do not evaluate persistence. We present Unfinished-Bench, a benchmark that gives models persistence in autonomous runs without a user, in realistic work environments with embedded rules. In each of its 97 scenarios across 7 organizations and 7 risk categories, the goal and the rule cannot both hold. We find that persistence raised violations of a rule in an internal document from 32.2% to 43.6%, five times the 8.5% for the same rule stated by the user. Of the document-rule violations, 98.3% occurred after the rule was displayed, and in 89% of a judged sample the agent first wrote down the rule. Violations of rules placed in the environment without user oversight can lead to serious incidents, so persistence-driven risks need to be evaluated. Benchmark documentation: https://francis21c.github.io/unfinished-bench/

</details>


### [81] [Diffusion Tree Sampling for Inference Time Adaptation of Crystal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17462)
*Daniel T. Levy,Vineet Jain,Tara Akhound-Sadegh,Sékou-Oumar Kaba,Siamak Ravanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在推理阶段用扩散树采样（DTS）对晶体结构扩散生成模型进行多目标引导，并给出两项扩展：把属性条件模型作为无偏引导提议，以及把DTS推广到多目标采样一次生成整条Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体生成任务需同时满足多个相互冲突的性质要求；微调代价高且每个新目标都要重训模型，可微引导又不适用于许多真实任务，因此需要一种不修改模型、在推理时即可引导的通用方法。

Method: 以Diffusion Tree Sampling（在去噪轨迹上的蒙特卡洛树搜索）为推理时适配框架，并提出：(1) 将已有的属性条件生成模型（如经classifier-free guidance微调的模型）用作DTS内部的引导提议，同时不扭曲目标分布；(2) 将DTS扩展为多目标采样，使单次搜索即可产生覆盖整条Pareto前沿的样本。在从头晶体生成上实例化这两种适配。

Result: 在多项任务上有效：提升不同模型生成晶体的稳定性；利用预训练条件模型生成稀有空间群或高体模量晶体；沿高介电常数与宽带隙的Pareto前沿生成晶体。

Conclusion: 基于DTS的推理时适配可在不修改模型本身的前提下稳定提升生成输出，为科学发现类生成任务提供了一种可组合、多目标的引导方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have become strong priors over plausible crystal structures, but turning that prior into promising candidates requires steering toward several conflicting properties at once. Fine-tuning is expensive and produces a new model for every new objective, while differentiable guidance is not applicable to many real-world tasks. We instead adapt these generative models at inference time using Diffusion Tree Sampling (DTS), a Monte Carlo tree search procedure over the denoising trajectory. We make two technical contributions on top of DTS that are particularly relevant for AI-for-science applications. First, we show how to use existing property-conditional generative models (e.g., models fine-tuned for classifier-free guidance) as guided proposals inside DTS, without distorting the target distribution. Second, we extend DTS to multi-objective sampling, so that a single search produces samples spanning an entire Pareto front. We instantiate both adaptations on de novo crystal generation: improving the stability of crystals generated by different models, using pretrained conditional models to generate crystals belonging to rare space groups or with high bulk modulus, and generating crystals along a Pareto front of high dielectric constant and high bandgap materials. Across these tasks, DTS-based inference-time adaptation successfully improves model outputs without modifying the models themselves.

</details>


### [82] [Availability-Aware Historical Feedback Learning for Electric Arc Furnace Temperature and Chemical Composition Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17463)
*Ahmed Shoyeb Raihan,Farzana Islam,Ashish Nimbarte,Imtiaz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种“可用性感知的历史反馈学习”框架，在考虑60分钟测量延迟的前提下，预测电弧炉出钢温度与12种元素终点成分；历史反馈特征在156组对比中147组降低RMSE，温度RMSE降至12.290 ℃（较仅时序特征降低5.54%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弧炉炼钢在再生钢与低碳钢生产中地位上升，但入炉物料、能量输入与操作条件波动大，且测量存在间歇与延迟，导致终点钢水温度与化学成分难以实时准确预测，可用于及时预测的信息受限。

Method: 整合8类工业数据源，构建19维静态、39维时序、47维历史信息增强的三级递进特征表示；仅在满足假定60分钟测量发布延迟条件下引入历史结果；使用19,738炉次温度数据与3,341炉次成分数据，对12种调优回归算法进行时间顺序前向验证；并开展历史特征消融、反馈延迟敏感性分析、轻量自适应残差校正及探索性深度学习对比与特征归因分析。

Result: 历史反馈表示在156组算法-目标匹配对比中147组降低RMSE；温度预测中ExtraTrees取得12.290 ℃的RMSE，较仅时序版本降低5.54%；消融与延迟敏感性实验表明近期结果信息及其可得性至关重要；在后段时期的回顾性评估中，自适应残差校正无需重训基础模型即将温度RMSE由12.502降至12.275 ℃。

Conclusion: 历史结果的可得性与顺序反馈是电弧炉终点预测的重要考量因素，可用性感知的历史反馈框架能有效提升温度与成分预测精度，并为在线应用提供可部署的轻量校正手段。

Abstract: Electric arc furnace (EAF) steelmaking plays an increasingly important role in recycled and lower-carbon steel production. However, variability in charged materials, energy input, and operating conditions makes accurate prediction of molten steel temperature and final chemical composition challenging. Intermittent or delayed measurements limit the information available for timely prediction. This study develops an availability-aware historical-feedback learning framework for predicting EAF endpoint temperature and the final concentrations of twelve chemical elements. Eight industrial data sources are integrated into three progressively enriched predictor representations comprising 19 static, 39 temporal, and 47 history-informed features. Historical outcomes are incorporated only when eligible under an assumed 60-minute measurement-release delay. Using 19,738 temperature heats and 3,341 chemistry heats, twelve tuned regression algorithms are evaluated through chronological forward validation. The proposed historical-feedback representation reduces root mean squared error (RMSE) in 147 of 156 matched algorithm-target comparisons. For temperature prediction, ExtraTrees achieves an RMSE of 12.290 degrees C, representing a 5.54 percent reduction compared with its temporal-only counterpart. Historical-feature ablation and feedback-delay sensitivity analyses demonstrate the importance of recent outcome information and its availability. In a separate retrospective later-period evaluation, lightweight adaptive residual correction reduces temperature RMSE from 12.502 to 12.275 degrees C without retraining the underlying model. Exploratory deep learning comparisons and target-specific feature-attribution analyses provide additional insights into predictive behavior. The results establish historical outcome availability and sequential feedback as important considerations for EAF endpoint prediction.

</details>


### [83] [Improved Regret via Variance-Aware Policy Optimization in Adversarial MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17469)
*Ofir Schlisselberg,Alon Cohen,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究未知动力学的分段式对抗性 MDP（全信息奖励），取得 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H^3SAK})$ 后悔界；提出"下游依赖"算法归约（未知→已知动力学），并用带 Stability-Matching Bonus 的 VOPO 策略优化算法实例化，同时给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有未知动力学对抗 MDP 的两个最优主导项互不可比，且与下界 $\widetilde O(H\sqrt{SAK})$ 存在差距；需要一个统一的分析框架来缩小甚至消除该差距。

Method: 1) 提出"下游依赖"算法的归约：策略在第 $h$ 步只依赖 $h+1$ 及之后的信息，从而把未知动力学问题归约为已知动力学问题；2) 实例化为 VOPO 策略优化算法，引入新颖的 Stability-Matching Bonus (SMB)，实现方差感知分析。
